**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/extract-objects-from-point-cloud.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_point_cloud", "label_zh": "輸入點云", "label_en": "Input Point Cloud", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "LAS Dataset Layer; Scene Layer; File", "description_en": "The classified LAS dataset, point cloud scene layer package, or point cloud I3S service that will be used to detect objects."}, {"parameter": "class_codes_to_extract", "label_zh": "要提取的類代碼", "label_en": "Class Codes To Extract", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The class codes that will be processed to identify objects. A common group ID can be assigned to group multiple class codes into the same object. Value table columns: Class Code—The class code value that will be used to identify objects. This number can be any value from 0 to 255. Group ID—The group ID that will be used to combine multiple class codes. This number can be any value from 0 to 255."}, {"parameter": "clustering_distance", "label_zh": "聚類距離", "label_en": "Clustering Distance", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The three-dimensional distance that will be used to identify the points that belong to the same object."}, {"parameter": "out_features", "label_zh": "輸出對象圖徵", "label_en": "Output Object Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "The output objects whose feature geometry may be point, polygon, or multipatch based on the Output Geometry Type parameter value."}, {"parameter": "geometry_type", "label_zh": "輸出幾何類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Geometry Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the type of geometry that will be created for each object. The specified type will determine whether the objects will be represented as points, polygons, or multipatch features. Centerpoint—Three-dimensional points will be generated that represent each object with a point that is created approximately in the middle of the object's convex hull. Bounding Circle—Two-dimensional polygons will be generated that represent each object with the minimum bounding circle that surrounds its points. 2D Bounding Box—Two-dimensional polygons will be generated that represent each object with the smallest oriented bounding box that contains its points. 2D Convex Hull—Two-dimensional polygons will be generated that represent each object with the convex hull that contains its points. 2D Concave Hull—Two-dimensional polygons will be generated that represent each object with a concave hull that encompasses its points. Bounding Sphere—Multipatch features will be generated that represent each object with a minimum bounding sphere that encompasses its points. 3D Bounding Box—Multipatch features will be generated that represent each object with the smallest oriented bounding box that contains its points. 3D Convex Hull—Multipatch features will be generated that represent each object with the convex hull that surrounds its points. This is the default. 3D Concave Hull—Multipatch features will be generated that represent each object with the concave hull that surrounds its points. This option approximates the closest fitting geometry that can be generated from the points."}, {"parameter": "min_points", "label_zh": "最小點數 （可選）", "label_en": "Minimum Number of Points (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The minimum number of points that an object must have for it to generate an output. The default value is 10."}, {"parameter": "vertical_scale_factor", "label_zh": "垂直聚類比例因子", "label_en": "Vertical Clustering Scale Factor (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The scale factor that will be applied to the z-values, which will impact how the clustering distance is applied along the z-axis and how points are clustered into objects. The default value is 1 with valid values ranging from 0.0 to 10.0. A scale factor of 0 will result in two-dimensional clustering; a scale factor that is smaller than 1 but larger than 0 will result in a larger z-range for each object. Likewise, a vertical scale factor that is greater than 1 will result in points along the z-axis being clustered over a shorter distance. The vertical scale factor only applies to how the points are clustered. The geometry of the points will not be modified and the z-units will not be altered."}, {"parameter": "alpha_calculation", "label_zh": "計算每個對象的唯一 alpha 半徑 （可選）", "label_en": "Calculate a unique alpha radius for each object (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the alpha radius will be calculated for each object or if the same alpha radius will be applied to all objects. This parameter is only enabled when the Output Geometry Type parameter is set to 2D Concave Hull or 2D Concave Hull. Checked—A unique alpha radius will be calculated for each object. Unchecked—The same alpha radius will be used for all objects. When this option is specified, the Alpha Radius parameter can be used to define this value. If no value is specified, an alpha radius will be calculated for the entire dataset. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "alpha_radius", "label_zh": "Alpha 半徑 （可選）", "label_en": "Alpha Radius (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The alpha radius that will be used to define the alpha shape when constructing the object features. This parameter applies when the Calculate a unique alpha radius for each object parameter is unchecked and the Output Geometry Type parameter is set to 2D Concave Hull or 3D Concave Hull. A larger alpha radius will create a more unified object feature with simpler boundaries until the point of reaching the convex hull, while smaller values will encompass the object's points with greater detail while also increasing the likelihood of producing disconnected parts. When no alpha radius is defined, the value is derived from the Clustering Distance and Vertical Clustering Scale Factor parameter values. See the Usage section in the tool help for more information about how the default alpha radius is derived."}, {"parameter": "min_height", "label_zh": "最小高度表面 （可選）", "label_en": "Minimum Height Surface (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Layer", "description_en": "The minimum height surface that will be used to filter the objects by height. Only the points that are above this surface will be represented in the output."}, {"parameter": "max_height", "label_zh": "最大高度表面 （可選）", "label_en": "Maximum Height Surface (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Layer", "description_en": "The maximum height surface that will be used to filter the output. Only the points that are below this surface will be represented in the output."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.3d.ExtractObjectsFromPointCloud(in_point_cloud, class_codes_to_extract, clustering_distance, out_features, {geometry_type}, {min_points}, {vertical_scale_factor}, {alpha_calculation}, {alpha_radius}, {min_height}, {max_height})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_point_cloud、class_codes_to_extract、clustering_distance、out_features、geometry_type、min_points、vertical_scale_factor、alpha_calculation、alpha_radius、min_height、max_height。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 插值點 (標準圖徵分析工具) · 匯總

**id**　c53d0f7716487a2562-1

**english**　Interpolate Points

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 標準圖徵分析工具箱
- 分析模式工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html

**text**　根據一組點的測量結果來預測新位置上的值。 此工具將各點處具有數值的點資料用作輸入，并根據預測值分類創建區域。
示例包括以下內容：
一處空氣質量管理區安裝有用于測量污染水平的傳感器。 此工具可用于預測未安裝傳感器位置的污染水平，如學校或醫院等高危人群聚集的地區。
根據從諸多單株植物中采集到的樣本來預測農作物內的重金屬濃度。
預測土壤養分水平（氮、磷、鉀等）和其他指標（如導電性），以便研究這些指標與作物產量的關系并規定田間各處的精確化肥用量。
氣象應用包括溫度、降雨和相關變量（例如酸雨）的預測。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html#summary

## 插值點 (標準圖徵分析工具) · 用法

**id**　c53d0f7716487a2562-2

**english**　Interpolate Points

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 標準圖徵分析工具箱
- 分析模式工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html

**text**　點圖層將用作輸入。 輸入圖層必須具有數值欄位才能作為插值的基礎。 此工具用于與溫度和污染級別等隨地形變化平緩的資料配合使用， 而不適用于人口或中位收入等在很短間隔范圍內變化明顯的資料。
可通過設置此工具來優化速度、精度或兩者平衡的組合。 預測越精確，計算結果的速度越慢，反之亦然。
可通過此工具利用輸出預測誤差選項來創建標準誤差的圖層。 插值圖層中 95% 的置信區間可通過計算出插值、然后加上兩個標準上限誤差并減去兩個標準下限誤差進行計算。
此工具使用 Esri 經驗貝葉斯克里金法來執行插值。 提供給此方法的參數受插值選項參數控制。 參數如下所示。 參數 速度 默認值 精度 資料變換類型 無 無 EMPIRICAL 半變異函數模型類型 POWER POWER K_BESSEL 各局部模型中的最大點數 50 75 200 局部模型區域重疊系數 1 1.5 3 模擬半變異函數的數量 30 100 200 最小相鄰圖徵數 8 10 15 最大相鄰圖徵數 8 10 15

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html#usage

## 插值點 (標準圖徵分析工具) · 參數

**id**　c53d0f7716487a2562-8

**english**　Interpolate Points

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 標準圖徵分析工具箱
- 分析模式工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html

**parameter**　outputPredictionError

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 輸出預測誤差 （可選）
- 指定是否將創建預測誤差輸出圖層。 選中參數后，將為插值預測輸出一個包含標準誤差的面圖層。 標準誤差可提供有關預測值的可信度的信息，因此非常有用。 一般來說，有 95% 的真值會落在兩個預測值標準誤差之間。 例如，一個新位置的預測值為 50，標準誤差為 5。 這意味著通過此任務預測出此地區的真值是 50，但不排除真值低至 40 或高至 60 的可能。 為計算合理值的范圍，可先用標準誤差乘以 2，然后將得出的值加上預測值來獲得范圍上限，再用預測值減去乘以 2 后得出的值來獲得范圍下限。 選中—將創建預測誤差輸出圖層。 未選中—不會創建預測誤差輸出圖層。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html#parameters

**id**　c53d0f7716487a2562-9

**english**　Interpolate Points

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 標準圖徵分析工具箱
- 分析模式工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html

**parameter**　classificationType

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 分類類型 （可選）
- 指定將預測值劃分到面的方法。 相等間隔—將以每個區域的密度值范圍相等的方式創建面。 幾何間隔—將基于具有幾何系列的分類間隔創建面。 這樣可確保每個類范圍與每個類中所擁有的值的數量大致相同，且間隔之間的變化一致。 這是默認設置。 相等面積—將以各個區域大小相等的方式創建面。 例如，如果結果的高密度值多于低密度值，則會為高密度創建更多面。 手動輸入分類間隔—將使用自定義區域值范圍。 這些值必須以分類間隔形式提供。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html#parameters

**english**　Interpolate Points

**interface**　python

- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- interpolateOption （可選）
- 指定將使用速度、精度還是兩者平衡的組合。 預測結果越精確，所花費的計算時間就越長。 1—將使用速度。 5—將使用速度和精度兩者平衡的組合。 這是默認設置。 9—將使用精度。
- String

## 插值點 (標準圖徵分析工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　c53d0f7716487a2562-22

**english**　Interpolate Points

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 標準圖徵分析工具箱
- 分析模式工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html

**parameter**　outputPredictionError

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- outputPredictionError （可選）
- 指定是否將創建預測誤差輸出圖層。 如果指定了 OUTPUT_ERROR，將為插值預測輸出一個包含標準誤差的面圖層。 標準誤差可提供有關預測值的可信度的信息，因此非常有用。 一般來說，有 95% 的真值會落在兩個預測值標準誤差之間。 例如，一個新位置的預測值為 50，標準誤差為 5。 這意味著通過此任務預測出此地區的真值是 50，但不排除真值低至 40 或高至 60 的可能。 為計算合理值的范圍，可先用標準誤差乘以 2，然后將得出的值加上預測值來獲得范圍上限，再用預測值減去乘以 2 后得出的值來獲得范圍下限。 OUTPUT_ERROR—將創建預測誤差輸出圖層。 NO_ERROR—不會創建預測誤差輸出圖層。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html#derived-output

## 插值點 (標準圖徵分析工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　c53d0f7716487a2562-23

**english**　Interpolate Points

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 標準圖徵分析工具箱
- 分析模式工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html

**parameter**　classificationType

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- classificationType （可選）
- 指定將預測值劃分到面的方法。 EQUALINTERVAL—將以每個區域的密度值范圍相等的方式創建面。 GEOMETRICINTERVAL—將基于具有幾何系列的分類間隔創建面。 這樣可確保每個類范圍與每個類中所擁有的值的數量大致相同，且間隔之間的變化一致。 這是默認設置。 EQUALAREA—將以各個區域大小相等的方式創建面。 例如，如果結果的高密度值多于低密度值，則會為高密度創建更多面。 MANUAL—將使用自定義區域值范圍。 這些值必須以分類間隔形式提供。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html#derived-output

## 插值點 (標準圖徵分析工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 標準圖徵分析工具箱
- 分析模式工具集

**english**　Interpolate Points

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html

**id**　c53d0f7716487a2562-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Interpolate Points，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入圖徵 [inputLayer]、輸出名稱 [outputName]、插值欄位 （可選） [field]、插值選項 （可選） [interpolateOption]、輸出預測誤差 （可選） [outputPredictionError]、分類類型 （可選） [classificationType]、類數目 （可選） [numClasses]、分類間隔 （可選） [classBreaks]、邊界面 （可選） [boundingPolygonLayer]、在點圖層上進行預測 （可選） [predictAtPointLayer]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html#parameters

## 插值點 (標準圖徵分析工具) · 英文版工具簽章與參數順序

**english**　Interpolate Points

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 標準圖徵分析工具箱
- 分析模式工具集

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html

**id**　c53d0f7716487a2562-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/feature-analysis/interpolate-points.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "inputLayer", "label_zh": "輸入圖徵", "label_en": "Input Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Set", "description_en": "The point features that will be interpolated to a continuous surface layer."}, {"parameter": "outputName", "label_zh": "輸出名稱", "label_en": "Output Name", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the output layer that will be created on your portal."}, {"parameter": "field", "label_zh": "插值欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Interpolation Field", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The numeric field containing the values that will be interpolated."}, {"parameter": "interpolateOption", "label_zh": "插值選項 （可選）", "label_en": "Interpolate Option (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies whether speed, accuracy, or a balanced combination of the two will be used. More accurate predictions take longer to calculate. Speed—Speed will be used. Balanced—A balanced combination of speed and accuracy will be used. This is the default. Accuracy—Accuracy will be used."}, {"parameter": "outputPredictionError", "label_zh": "輸出預測誤差 （可選）", "label_en": "Output prediction error (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether a prediction error output layer will be created. If the parameter is checked, a polygon layer of standard errors for the interpolation predictions will be output. Standard errors are useful because they provide information about the reliability of the predicted values. A simple rule of thumb is that the true value will fall within two standard errors of the predicted value 95 percent of the time. For example, a new location has a predicted value of 50 with a standard error of 5. This means that this task's best prediction is that the true value at that location is 50, but it reasonably could be as low as 40 or as high as 60. To calculate this range of reasonable values, multiply the standard error by 2, add this value to the predicted value to get the upper end of the range, and subtract it from the predicted value to get the lower end of the range. Checked—A prediction error output layer will be created. Unchecked—A prediction error output layer will not be created. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "classificationType", "label_zh": "分類類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Classification Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies how predicted values will be classified into polygons. Equal interval—Polygons will be created so that the range of density values is equal for each area. Geometric interval—Polygons will be created based on class intervals that have a geometric series. This ensures that each class range has approximately the same number of values in each class and that the change between intervals is consistent. This is the default. Equal area—Polygons will be created so that the size of each area is equal. For example, if the result has more high-density values than low-density values, more polygons will be created for high densities. Enter class breaks manually—A custom range of values for areas will be used. These values must be provided as class breaks."}, {"parameter": "numClasses", "label_zh": "類數目 （可選）", "label_en": "Number of Classes (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The range of predicted values that will be divided into distinct classes. The range of values in each class is determined by the classification type. Each class defines the boundaries of the result polygons. The default is 10 and the maximum is 32."}, {"parameter": "classBreaks", "label_zh": "分類間隔 （可選）", "label_en": "Class Breaks (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The class break values that will be used for a manual classification. These values define the upper limit of each class, so the number of classes will equal the number of values provided. Areas will not be created for any locations with predicted values above the largest break value provided. You must provide at least 2 and no more than 32 values."}, {"parameter": "boundingPolygonLayer", "label_zh": "邊界面 （可選）", "label_en": "Bounding Polygons (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Set", "description_en": "The layer containing the polygons where values will be interpolated. For example, if you are interpolating densities of fish in a lake, you can use the boundary of the lake in this parameter, and the output will only contain polygons within the boundary of the lake."}, {"parameter": "predictAtPointLayer", "label_zh": "在點圖層上進行預測 （可選）", "label_en": "Predict At Point Layer (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Set", "description_en": "An optional layer containing point locations that will be used to calculate prediction values. This allows you to make predictions at specific locations of interest. For example, if the input layer represents measurements of pollution levels, you can use this parameter to predict the pollution levels of locations with large at-risk populations, such as schools or hospitals. You can then use this information to give recommendations to health officials in those locations."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.sfa.InterpolatePoints(inputLayer, outputName, field, {interpolateOption}, {outputPredictionError}, {classificationType}, {numClasses}, {classBreaks}, {boundingPolygonLayer}, {predictAtPointLayer})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：inputLayer、outputName、field、interpolateOption、outputPredictionError、classificationType、numClasses、classBreaks、boundingPolygonLayer、predictAtPointLayer。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

**text**　雖然此錯誤可能會發生，但發生的頻率非常低，以至于我們尚且無法確定典型原因，也因此目前沒有可用的解決方案。
如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

**id**　c543ac5636eb5aec3b-1

**english**　010372: The centroid of zone <value1> is covered by that of zone <value2>.

**chapter**

- 工具錯誤和警告

**text**　<value1> 中指定的區域質心與 <value2> 中指定的區域質心位于同一座標位置（重合）。 由于只能維護一個位置，因此系統發送該消息以指示此類情況。 質心位置值將采用最低區域值對應的值。

## 010072: 無法創建哈希表。 · 描述

**id**　c552abee52ae492e72-1

**english**　010072: Unable to create a hash table.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 10001-10100

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/010001-020000/tool-errors-and-warnings-10051-10075-010072.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/010001-020000/tool-errors-and-warnings-10051-10075-010072.html

**text**　可用內存不足，無法處理該操作。 某些操作會生成內部表（即哈希表），以執行創建輸出所需的計算。 對于較大或復雜的輸入，這些哈希表可以獲取的內存大于系統的可用內存，從而導致錯誤狀況。 另請注意，同時在系統上運行多個應用程序會減少 ArcGIS 的可用內存數量。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**id**　c552abee52ae492e72-2

**english**　010072: Unable to create a hash table.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 10001-10100

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/010001-020000/tool-errors-and-warnings-10051-10075-010072.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/010001-020000/tool-errors-and-warnings-10051-10075-010072.html

**text**　可以考慮執行以下一些選項：
關閉不需要的應用程序以釋放系統內存。
通過更改虛擬內存設置配置更多交換空間。
Esri 格網網格中可以存在的唯一值數量有限。 如果您具有的格網接近此限值（大約 21 億個唯一值），將其裁剪為較小的片段，并單獨處理每個片段。 然后使用 鑲嵌 將結果合并成最終輸出。

## 分類 LAS 地面點 (3D Analyst 工具) · 用法

**id**　c585d93aaf8cfe72b9-2

**english**　Classify LAS Ground

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 點云工具集
- 分類工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-ground.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-ground.html

**text**　此工具要求輸入 LAS 資料集具有投影座標系統。 通過將 提取 LAS 工具與輸出座標系環境設置中指定的投影座標系統結合使用，可以對地理座標系中存儲的資料進行重新投影。 如果投影座標系統基于不同的基準面，則應指定基準面變換。
只有類別代碼值為 0、1 或 2 的 LAS 點可以被分配為地面點。 如果您的 LAS 文件使用不同的類代碼值來表示未分類或地面測量值，請使用 更改 LAS 類代碼 工具對其進行相應的重新分配。 分類過程還將忽略分配有重疊或保留分類標記的點。
標準分類方法通常能滿足大多數資料集的地面點檢測需求。 如果檢測到的地面點中包含較多非地面點，建議使用保守分類方法來減少這種誤差。 相反，如果資料集中包含具有陡坡圖徵的崎嶇地形，且可能被植被部分遮擋，建議采用激進分類方法，以捕捉更多的地面點。 若使用該選項后仍有部分山脊未被識別，可通過空間范圍或面邊界劃定改進區域，并啟用恢復山脊選項重新運行工具。
由于帶有橋梁和高速公路入口匝道的位置可能會被錯誤分類為地面，因此應對這些位置進行檢查。 要糾正這些區域，建議應用處理范圍或邊界，隔離錯誤分類區域及其周圍的部分地形，然后再次運行工具。 隔離的區域應盡量避開橋梁或匝道與地面連接的部分。
如果您需要比正常資料集處理時間更快地獲取地面分類結果，可以使用 DEM 分辨率參數。 該參數可確保分類地面點的數量足以生成指定分辨率的地面網格表面，但分類地面點數量將少于常規處理。
對模型關鍵點進行分類將得到一個地面點子集，可用于替代全分辨率地面點。 當地面樣本密度高于給定應用所需密度時，這將非常有用。 與提供快速地面分類方法的 DEM 分辨率參數不同，對模型關鍵點進行分類可以更完整地分類地面點，并提供一個可用于下游操作（例如創建高程網格表面）的子集。 在 LAS 文件版本 1.0 中，模型關鍵點將被分配類代碼值 8；在所有其他 LAS 文件版本中，模型關鍵點將保留類代碼值 2，但會分配模型關鍵點分類標志。 了解關于如何使用激光雷達創建地面高程網格的詳細信息
標記為保留或重疊的點將被忽略。 保留點最初被定義為不用于分類。 重疊點在處理過程中被忽略，因為包含它們會造成點密度的劇烈波動，并且它們還可能在航線之間引入與噪點相關的校準問題。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-ground.html#usage

## 分類 LAS 地面點 (3D Analyst 工具) · 參數

**id**　c585d93aaf8cfe72b9-5

**english**　Classify LAS Ground

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 點云工具集
- 分類工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-ground.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-ground.html

**parameter**　method

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 地面檢測方法
- 指定將用于檢測地面點的方法。 Standard Classification—This method has a tolerance for slope variation that allows it to capture gradual undulations in the ground's topography that would typically be missed by the Conservative Classification option but not capture the type of sharp reliefs that would be captured by the Aggressive Classification option. This is the default. Conservative Classification—When compared to other options, this method uses a tighter restriction on the variation of the ground's slope that allows it to differentiate the ground from low-lying vegetation such as grass and shrubbery. It is best suited for topography with minimal curvature. Aggressive Classification—This method detects ground areas with sharper reliefs, such as ridges and hilltops, that may be ignored by the Standard Classification option. This method is best used in a second iteration of this tool with the Reuse existing ground parameter checked. Avoid using this method in urban areas or flat, rural areas, as it may result in the misclassification of taller objects—such as utility towers, vegetation, and portions of buildings—as ground. Recover Ridges—This method detects ridges that may be undetected by the Aggressive Classification option. A processing extent or boundary polygon must be provided to use this method. Model Key Classification—This method classifies a subset of existing ground points as model key. For this reason, the ground points must already be classified. This option provides a faster way to achieve model key classification instead of reclassifying all the ground points. In LAS versions 1.0 to 1.3, the ground points that are identified as model key will be assigned a class code value of 7. LAS version 1.4 files will maintain the ground class code of 2 and have the model key classification flag assigned.
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-ground.html#parameters

**id**　c585d93aaf8cfe72b9-9

**english**　Classify LAS Ground

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 點云工具集
- 分類工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-ground.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-ground.html

**parameter**　extent

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 處理范圍 （可選）
- 將評估的資料范圍。 當前顯示范圍 - 范圍基于活動地圖或場景。 繪制范圍 - 范圍將基于在地圖或場景上繪制的矩形。 圖層范圍 - 范圍基于活動地圖圖層。 選擇可用圖層或者使用所有圖層中的資料范圍選項。 每個地圖圖層具有以下選項： 所有圖徵 - 所有圖徵的范圍。 選定圖徵 - 選定圖徵的范圍。 可見圖徵 - 可見圖徵的范圍。 瀏覽 - 范圍將基于資料集。 輸入的交集 - 范圍將為所有輸入的相交范圍。 輸入的并集 - 范圍將為所有輸入的合并范圍。 剪貼板 - 可在剪貼板中復制和粘貼范圍。 復制范圍 - 將范圍座標和座標系復制到剪貼板。 粘貼范圍 - 從剪貼板粘貼范圍和座標系。 如果剪貼板不包含座標系，則范圍將使用地圖的座標系。 重置范圍 - 將范圍重置為默認值。 當手動提供座標時，座標必須為數值，并且位于活動地圖的座標系中。 地圖可能使用與提供的座標不同的顯示單位。 在南緯和西經座標中使用負號。
- Extent

- 指定是將保留類代碼為 7 的現有低噪聲點，還是對其進行重新分類。 如果對低噪聲點進行重新分類，則對于任何未低于地面至少為地下深度下限參數值提供的距離的點，將為其分配類代碼值 1。 選中—Existing low-noise points will be preserved. This is the default. 未選中—Existing low-noise points will be reclassified.
- Boolean

- 指定用于對模型關鍵點進行分類的方法。 Z Tolerance—Model-key points will be selected to ensure that the TIN surface derived from the model-key points is within a given height range of the TIN surface derived from the complete set of ground points. This is the default. Window Size—Model-key points will be derived using the window-size thinning method, where the data is divided into a grid whose resolution is set by the Window Size parameter value. Each grid will have one ground point selected based on the option specified for the Window Size Point Selection Method parameter.

- 指定在使用窗口大小方法時用于選擇地面點子集的高度標準。 窗口大小子采樣技術的每個圖格將有一個點。 與所選方法對應的點將用于模型關鍵點的檢測。 Average—The point whose z-value is closest to the average of all points in the window size bin will be used. This is the default. Maximum—The point with the highest z-value in the window size bin will be used. Minimum—The point with the lowest z-value in the window size bin will be used.

## 分類 LAS 地面點 (3D Analyst 工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　c585d93aaf8cfe72b9-28

**english**　Classify LAS Ground

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 點云工具集
- 分類工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-ground.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-ground.html

**parameter**　method

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- method
- 指定將用于檢測地面點的方法。 STANDARD—This method typically offers the best results for most landscapes, as it has a tolerance for slope variation that allows it to detect the ground on a broad variety of landscapes. It will capture more ground points than the CONSERVATIVE option but may miss ground points on terrains with steep slope characteristics. This is the default. CONSERVATIVE—When compared to other options, this method uses a tighter restriction on the variation of the ground's slope that allows it to differentiate the ground from low-lying vegetation such as grass and shrubbery. It is best suited for topography with minimal curvature. AGGRESSIVE—This method is best suited for ground detection over rugged terrain. Avoid using this method in terrain that contains relatively flat slope profiles, as it may misclassify low vegetation points and human-constructed objects as ground. RECOVER_RIDGES—This method detects ground points along ridges that were undetected by a preceding classification. It is intended for use in isolated areas where ground points on rugged terrain were not adequately detected. A processing extent or boundary polygon must be provided to use this method. MODEL_KEY—This method classifies model-key points from a subset of existing ground points. This option provides a faster way to classify model-key points for datasets where ground points are already classified. In LAS file versions 1.0 to 1.3, the ground points that are identified as model key will be assigned a class code value of 7. LAS version 1.4 files will maintain the ground class code of 2 and have the model key classification flag assigned.
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-ground.html#derived-output

**values**　
- reuse_ground （可選）
- 指定現有地面點是否會重新分類或重新使用。 REUSE_GROUND—Existing ground points will be accepted and reused without scrutiny and contribute to the determination of unclassified points. RECLASSIFY_GROUND—Existing ground points will be reclassified. Points that are not found to be a part of the ground will be reassigned a class code value of 1, which represents unclassified points. This is the default.
- Boolean

- 將導致僅評估分類為地面的點子集，從而加快分類過程的距離。 需要更迅速地生成 DEM 表面時可考慮使用該參數。 最小距離為 0.3 米，但提供的距離必須至少為激光雷達資料平均點間距的 1.5 倍，否則該過程將無法生效。

- 指定是否將計算 LAS 資料集引用的 .las 文件的統計資料。 計算統計資料時會為每個 .las 文件提供一個空間索引，從而提高了分析和顯示性能。 統計資料還可通過將 LAS 屬性（如分類代碼和回波信息）顯示限制為 .las 文件中存在的值來提升過濾和符號系統體驗。 COMPUTE_STATS—Statistics will be computed. This is the default. NO_COMPUTE_STATS—Statistics will not be computed.

- 指定是否將模型關鍵點與地面點一起分類。 當此參數設置為 CLASSIFY_KEY_POINTS 時，LAS 文件版本 1.0 中的模型關鍵點將被分配類代碼值 8，并且所有其他 LAS 文件版本中的模型關鍵點將保留類代碼 2，但會分配模型關鍵點分類標志。 當 method 參數值為 MODEL_KEY 時，將強制執行此選項。 CLASSIFY_KEY_POINTS—Model-key points will be classified. NO_CLASSIFY_KEY_POINTS—Model-key points will not be classified. This is the default.

- 說明

**values**　
- classification_type （可選）
- 指定用于對模型關鍵點進行分類的方法。 Z_TOLERANCE—Model-key points will be the minimum number of points required to ensure that the TIN surface derived from the model-key points is within a given height range of the TIN surface derived from the complete set of ground points. This is the default. WINDOW_SIZE—Model-key points will be derived using the window-size thinning method, where the data is divided into a grid whose resolution is set by the window_size_ parameter value. Each grid will have one ground point selected based on the option specified for the window_size_method parameter.
- String

- 窗口大小子采樣技術將使用的分辨率。 當 classification_type 參數值為 Z_TOLERANCE 時，窗口大小將用于確保實現最小樣本密度，這可以避免在資料中創建可能導致某些類型分析出現問題的大間隙。 要忽略此過程，請將該參數留空或提供一個小于或等于 0.0 的值。

**id**　c585d93aaf8cfe72b9-47

**english**　Classify LAS Ground

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 點云工具集
- 分類工具集

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-ground.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-ground.html

**parameter**　window_size_method

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- window_size_method （可選）
- 指定在使用窗口大小方法時用于選擇地面點子集的高度標準。 窗口大小子采樣技術的每個圖格將有一個點。 與所選方法對應的點將用于模型關鍵點的檢測。 AVERAGE—The point whose z-value is closest to the average of all points in the window size bin will be used. This is the default. MAXIMUM—The point with the highest z-value in the window size bin will be used. MINIMUM—The point with the lowest z-value in the window size bin will be used.
- String

## 分類 LAS 地面點 (3D Analyst 工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　c585d93aaf8cfe72b9-52

**english**　Classify LAS Ground

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 3D Analyst 工具箱
- 點云工具集
- 分類工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-ground.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-ground.html

**text**　'''****************************************************************************
Name:        Classify Ground & Vegetation in Forest Environment
Description: Classify points representing vegetation with LAS class code values
             of 3, 4, and 5. The code is designed for use as a script tool.
****************************************************************************'''
# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set Local Variables
inLas = arcpy.GetParameterAsText(0)
recursion = arcpy.GetParameterAsText(1)
lasd = arcpy.GetParameterAsText(2)

try:
    # Run CreateLasDataset
    arcpy.management.CreateLasDataset(inLas, lasd, folder_recursion=recursion)
    # Make an initial pass of ground classifier
    arcpy.ddd.ClassifyLasGround(lasd, method="Conservative")
    # Make a secondary pass to capture ridges
    arcpy.ddd.ClassifyLasGround(lasd, method="Aggressive",
                                reuse_ground="REUSE_GROUND")
    # Classify vegetation
    arcpy.ddd.ClassifyLasByHeight(lasd, ground_source='GROUND',
                                  height_classification=[[3, 5],
                                                         [4, 17],
                                                         [5, 120]],
                                  noise='HIGH_NOISE', compute_stats="COMPUTE_STATS")

except arcpy.ExecuteError:
    print(arcpy.GetMessages())

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-ground.html#derived-output

## 分類 LAS 地面點 (3D Analyst 工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具

**english**　Classify LAS Ground

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-ground.html

**id**　c585d93aaf8cfe72b9-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/3d-analyst/classify-las-ground.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Classify LAS Ground，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入 LAS 資料集 [in_las_dataset]、地面檢測方法 [method]、重用現有地面 （可選） [reuse_ground]、DEM 分辨率 （可選） [dem_resolution]、計算統計資料 （可選） [compute_stats]、處理范圍 （可選） [extent]、處理邊界 [boundary]、處理與范圍相交的整個 LAS 文件 （可選） [process_entire_files]、更新金字塔 （可選） [update_pyramid]、檢測算法 （可選） [algorithm]、分類低噪點 （可選） [classify_low_noise]、地下深度下限 （可選） [minimum_depth_below_ground]、保留現有低噪點 （可選） [preserve_low_noise]、分類高噪點 （可選） [classify_high_noise]、地上高度下限 （可選） [minimum_height_above_ground]、保留現有高噪點 （可選） [preserve_high_noise]、分類模型關鍵點 （可選） [classify_key_points]、分類類型 （可選） [classification_type]、Z 容差 （可選） [z_tolerance]、窗口大小 （可選） [window_size]、窗口大小點選擇方法 （可選） [window_size_method]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_las_dataset", "label_zh": "輸入 LAS 資料集", "label_en": "Input LAS Dataset", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "LAS Dataset Layer", "description_en": "The LAS dataset that will be processed. Only the last return of LAS points with class code values of 0, 1, and 2 will be evaluated."}, {"parameter": "method", "label_zh": "地面檢測方法", "label_en": "Ground Detection Method (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the method that will be used to detect ground points. Standard Classification—This method has a tolerance for slope variation that allows it to capture gradual undulations in the ground's topography that would typically be missed by the Conservative Classification option but not capture the type of sharp reliefs that would be captured by the Aggressive Classification option. This is the default. Conservative Classification—When compared to other options, this method uses a tighter restriction on the variation of the ground's slope that allows it to differentiate the ground from low-lying vegetation such as grass and shrubbery. It is best suited for topography with minimal curvature. Aggressive Classification—This method detects ground areas with sharper reliefs, such as ridges and hilltops, that may be ignored by the Standard Classification option. This method is best used in a second iteration of this tool with the Reuse existing ground parameter checked. Avoid using this method in urban areas or flat, rural areas, as it may result in the misclassification of taller objects—such as utility towers, vegetation, and portions of buildings—as ground. Recover Ridges—This method detects ridges that may be undetected by the Aggressive Classification option. A processing extent or boundary polygon must be provided to use this method. Model Key Classification—This method classifies a subset of existing ground points as model key. For this reason, the ground points must already be classified. This option provides a faster way to achieve model key classification instead of reclassifying all the ground points. In LAS versions 1.0 to 1.3, the ground points that are identified as model key will be assigned a class code value of 7. LAS version 1.4 files will maintain the ground class code of 2 and have the model key classification flag assigned."}, {"parameter": "reuse_ground", "label_zh": "重用現有地面 （可選）", "label_en": "Reuse existing ground (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether existing ground points will be reclassified or reused. Checked—Existing ground points will be accepted and reused without scrutiny and contribute to the determination of unclassified points. Unchecked—Existing ground points will be reclassified. Points that are not found to be a part of the ground will be reassigned a class code value of 1, which represents unclassified points. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "dem_resolution", "label_zh": "DEM 分辨率 （可選）", "label_en": "DEM Resolution (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "A distance that will result in only a subset of points being evaluated for classification as ground, making the process faster. Consider using this parameter when a faster method for generating a DEM surface is needed. The minimum distance is 0.3 meters, but the provided distance must be at least 1.5 times the average point spacing of the lidar data for this process to take effect."}, {"parameter": "compute_stats", "label_zh": "計算統計資料 （可選）", "label_en": "Compute statistics (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether statistics will be computed for the .las files referenced by the LAS dataset. Computing statistics provides a spatial index for each .las file, which improves analysis and display performance. Statistics also enhance the filtering and symbology experience by limiting the display of LAS attributes, such as classification codes and return information, to values that are present in the .las file. Checked—Statistics will be computed. This is the default. Unchecked—Statistics will not be computed."}, {"parameter": "extent", "label_zh": "處理范圍 （可選）", "label_en": "Processing Extent (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Extent", "description_en": "The extent of the data that will be evaluated. Current Display Extent —The extent will be based on the active map or scene. Draw Extent —The extent will be based on a rectangle drawn on the map or scene. Extent of a Layer —The extent will be based on an active map layer. Choose an available layer or use the Extent of data in all layers option. Each map layer has the following options: All Features —The extent of all features. Selected Features —The extent of the selected features. Visible Features —The extent of visible features. Browse —The extent will be based on a dataset. Intersection of Inputs —The extent will be the intersecting extent of all inputs. Union of Inputs —The extent will be the combined extent of all inputs. Clipboard —The extent can be copied to and from the clipboard. Copy Extent —Copies the extent and coordinate system to the clipboard. Paste Extent —Pastes the extent and coordinate system from the clipboard. If the clipboard does not include a coordinate system, the extent will use the map's coordinate system. Reset Extent —The extent will be reset to the default value. When coordinates are manually provided, the coordinates must be numeric values and in the active map's coordinate system. The map may use different display units than the provided coordinates. Use a negative value sign for south and west coordinates."}, {"parameter": "boundary", "label_zh": "處理邊界", "label_en": "Processing Boundary (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The polygon feature or features that will define the area to be processed."}, {"parameter": "process_entire_files", "label_zh": "處理與范圍相交的整個 LAS 文件 （可選）", "label_en": "Process entire LAS files that intersect extent (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies how the processing extent will be applied. Checked—If any portion of a .las file intersects the area of interest, all the points in that file, including those outside the area of interest, will be processed. Unchecked—Only LAS points that intersect the area of interest will be processed. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "update_pyramid", "label_zh": "更新金字塔 （可選）", "label_en": "Update pyramid (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the LAS dataset pyramid will be updated after the class codes are modified. Checked—The LAS dataset pyramid will be updated. This is the default. Unchecked—The LAS dataset pyramid will not be updated."}, {"parameter": "algorithm", "label_zh": "檢測算法 （可選）", "label_en": "Detection Algorithm (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the version of the ground detection algorithm that will be used to classify the ground points. Latest—The most recent version of the ground detection algorithm will be used. This option improves the handling of noise and outlier points, especially for photogrammetrically derived point clouds. It also produces better results and faster performance in most cases. This is the default. First Generation—The initial version of the ground detection algorithm will be used. Use this option only when the results from the latest version are not suitable."}, {"parameter": "classify_low_noise", "label_zh": "分類低噪點 （可選）", "label_en": "Classify low-noise points (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether points below a given distance beneath the ground will be classified as low noise. The distance at which noise points are identified is based on the Minimum Depth Below Ground parameter value. Low-noise points are assigned a class code value of 7. Checked—Low-noise points will be classified. Unchecked—Low-noise points will not be classified. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "minimum_depth_below_ground", "label_zh": "地下深度下限 （可選）", "label_en": "Minimum Depth Below Ground (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The distance below the ground that will be used to classify low-noise points. The ground will be defined by a triangulated surface created from ground-classified points. All points with class codes 0 or 1 that are below the ground by the height provided in this parameter will be assigned a class code value of 7."}, {"parameter": "preserve_low_noise", "label_zh": "保留現有低噪點 （可選）", "label_en": "Preserve existing low noise (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether existing low noise points with class code 7 will be preserved or reclassified. If low-noise points are reclassified, any points that are not below the ground by at least the distance provided for the Minimum Depth Below Ground parameter value will be assigned a class code value of 1. Checked—Existing low-noise points will be preserved. This is the default. Unchecked—Existing low-noise points will be reclassified."}, {"parameter": "classify_high_noise", "label_zh": "分類高噪點 （可選）", "label_en": "Classify high-noise points (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether points above a given distance from the ground will be classified as high noise. The distance at which noise points are identified is based on the Minimum Height Above Ground parameter value. High-noise points are assigned a class code value of 18. Checked—High-noise points will be classified. Unchecked—High-noise points will not be classified. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "minimum_height_above_ground", "label_zh": "地上高度下限 （可選）", "label_en": "Minimum Height Above Ground (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The distance above the ground that will be used to classify high-noise points. The ground will be defined by a triangulated surface created from ground-classified points. All points with class codes 0 or 1 that are above the ground by the height provided in this parameter will be assigned a class code value of 18."}, {"parameter": "preserve_high_noise", "label_zh": "保留現有高噪點 （可選）", "label_en": "Preserve existing high noise (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether existing high-noise points with class code 18 will be preserved or reclassified. If high-noise points are reclassified, any points that are not above the ground by at least the distance provided for the Minimum Height Above Ground parameter will be assigned a class code value of 1. Checked—Existing high-noise points will be preserved. This is the default. Unchecked—Existing high-noise points will be reclassified."}, {"parameter": "classify_key_points", "label_zh": "分類模型關鍵點 （可選）", "label_en": "Classify model-key points (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether model-key points will be classified with the ground points. When this parameter is checked, model-key points in LAS file version 1.0 will be assigned a class code value of 8, and model-key points in all other LAS file versions will remain with class code 2 but have the model-key classification flag assigned. This option is enforced when the Ground Detection Method parameter value is Model Key Classification. Checked—Model-key points will be classified. Unchecked—Model-key points will not be classified. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "classification_type", "label_zh": "分類類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Classification Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the method that will be used to classify model-key points. Z Tolerance—Model-key points will be selected to ensure that the TIN surface derived from the model-key points is within a given height range of the TIN surface derived from the complete set of ground points. This is the default. Window Size—Model-key points will be derived using the window-size thinning method, where the data is divided into a grid whose resolution is set by the Window Size parameter value. Each grid will have one ground point selected based on the option specified for the Window Size Point Selection Method parameter."}, {"parameter": "z_tolerance", "label_zh": "Z 容差 （可選）", "label_en": "Z Tolerance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The maximum height deviation that will be allowed between the TIN surface created from the model-key points and the TIN created from the complete set of ground points. The larger the parameter value is, the smaller the number of model-key points will be."}, {"parameter": "window_size", "label_zh": "窗口大小 （可選）", "label_en": "Window Size (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The resolution that will be used by the window size sub-sampling technique. When the Classification Type parameter value is Z Tolerance, the window size will be used to ensure a minimal sample density is achieved which can avoid the creation of large gaps in the data that can cause issues for certain types of analysis. To ignore this process leave the parameter unspecified or provide a value that is less than or equal to 0.0."}, {"parameter": "window_size_method", "label_zh": "窗口大小點選擇方法 （可選）", "label_en": "Window Size Point Selection Method (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the height based criteria that will be used to select a subset of ground points when using the window size method. Each bin of the window-size sub-sampling technique will have one point. The point that corresponds with the selected method will be used in the detection of model-key points. Average—The point whose z-value is closest to the average of all points in the window size bin will be used. This is the default. Maximum—The point with the highest z-value in the window size bin will be used. Minimum—The point with the lowest z-value in the window size bin will be used."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.3d.ClassifyLasGround(in_las_dataset, {method}, {reuse_ground}, {dem_resolution}, {compute_stats}, {extent}, {boundary}, {process_entire_files}, {update_pyramid}, {algorithm}, {classify_low_noise}, {minimum_depth_below_ground}, {preserve_low_noise}, {classify_high_noise}, {minimum_height_above_ground}, {preserve_high_noise}, {classify_key_points}, {classification_type}, {z_tolerance}, {window_size}, {window_size_method})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_las_dataset、method、reuse_ground、dem_resolution、compute_stats、extent、boundary、process_entire_files、update_pyramid、algorithm、classify_low_noise、minimum_depth_below_ground、preserve_low_noise、classify_high_noise、minimum_height_above_ground、preserve_high_noise、classify_key_points、classification_type、z_tolerance、window_size、window_size_method。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 方向分布 (標準差橢圓) (空間統計工具) · 匯總

**id**　c589c21e8e82715d7f-1

**english**　Directional Distribution (Standard Deviational Ellipse)

**text**　創建標準差橢圓或橢圓體來匯總地理圖徵的空間特征：中心趨勢、離散和方向趨勢。
了解“方向分布（標準差橢圓）”工作原理

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/directional-distribution.html#summary

## 方向分布 (標準差橢圓) (空間統計工具) · 用法

**id**　c589c21e8e82715d7f-2

**english**　Directional Distribution (Standard Deviational Ellipse)

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 度量地理分布工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/directional-distribution.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/directional-distribution.html

**text**　在此工具所創建的輸出中，每個案例（如果指定案例分組欄位參數）都對應一個橢圓面或 3D 橢圓多面體。 這些橢圓面的屬性值會包括平均中心的 x 和 y 座標、兩個標準距離（長軸和短軸）及橢圓的方向。 當使用已啟用 z 座標的點資料時，屬性值也會包含平均中心的 z 座標、第三個標準距離（高度）以及橢圓體的方位測量值。 如果輸入圖徵是二維的，欄位名稱將為 CenterX、CenterY、XStdDist、YStdDist 和 Rotation。 如果輸入圖徵是 3D 點，以下欄位也將添加到輸出中：CenterZ、ZStdDist、AngleZ、TiltX、RollY 和 Volume。 當提供了案例分組欄位參數值時，此欄位也將添加到輸出中。
此工具支持點資料的 3D 特性，在提供 z 值的情況下，將在其計算中使用 x、y 和 z 值。 因為這些結果本質上是 3D 圖徵，因此需要在場景中進行可視化。 要正確可視化分析結果，請確保在場景中運行分析或將結果圖層復制到場景中。
計算需要使用投影資料來準確測量距離。
如果圖徵的基礎空間模式是朝中心集中而朝向外圍的圖徵較少（遵循空間高斯分布），則一個標準差橢圓面會包含聚類中約 68％ 的圖徵，兩個標準差橢圓面會包含聚類中約 95％ 的圖徵，三個標準差橢圓面則可包含約 99％ 的圖徵。
如果輸入資料啟用了 z 值，那么輸出欄位 AngleZ、TiltX 和 RollY 的值為歐拉角度，用于指示橢圓體在 3D 空間中的方位。 如果資料沒有啟用 Z，那么輸出欄位 Rotation 中的值表示長軸從頂點開始按順時針測量的旋轉角度。
您可以使用計算幾何屬性工具來查看您資料的 x、y 和 z 座標。
案例分組欄位參數用于在分析前對圖徵進行分組。 當指定了案例分組欄位參數值時，系統會首先根據案例分組欄位值對輸入圖徵進行分組， 然后計算每個組的標準差橢圓或橢圓體。 案例分組欄位可以為整型、日期型或字符串型。 對于案例分組欄位參數值，具有空值的記錄將從分析中排除。
可以根據可選的權重欄位參數計算標準差橢圓或橢圓體（例如，獲取按嚴重程度衡量的交通事故的橢圓）。 權重欄位應該是數值。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/directional-distribution.html#usage

## 方向分布 (標準差橢圓) (空間統計工具) · 用法

**id**　c589c21e8e82715d7f-3

**english**　Directional Distribution (Standard Deviational Ellipse)

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 度量地理分布工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/directional-distribution.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/directional-distribution.html

**text**　對于線和面圖徵，距離計算中會使用圖徵的質心。 對于多點、折線或由多部分組成的面，將會使用所有圖徵部分的加權平均中心來計算質心。 點圖徵的加權項是 1；線圖徵的加權項是長度；而面圖徵的加權項是面積。
地圖圖層可用于定義輸入圖徵類。 使用帶選區的圖層時，只有選定的圖徵才會包含在分析中。 注意： 在使用 shapefile 時，請注意 shapefile 無法存儲空值。 根據非 shapefile 輸入創建 shapefile 的工具或其他過程可能會將空值存儲（或解釋）為零。 某些情況下，空值則以極大的負值儲存于 shapefile 中。 這會產生意外的結果。 有關詳細信息，請參閱 shapefile 輸出的地理處理注意事項。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/directional-distribution.html#usage

## 方向分布 (標準差橢圓) (空間統計工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　c589c21e8e82715d7f-19

**english**　Directional Distribution (Standard Deviational Ellipse)

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 度量地理分布工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/directional-distribution.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/directional-distribution.html

**text**　# Measure the geographic distribution of auto thefts.

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Local variables...
workspace = "C:/data"
locations = "AutoTheft.shp"
links = "AutoTheft_links.shp"
standardDistance = "auto_theft_SD.shp"
stardardEllipse = "auto_theft_SE.shp"
linearDirectMean = "auto_theft_LDM.shp"

# Set the workspace (to avoid having to type in the full path to the data every
# time).
arcpy.env.workspace = workspace

# Process: Standard Distance of auto theft locations
arcpy.stats.StandardDistance(locations, standardDistance, "1_STANDARD_DEVIATION")

# Process: Directional Distribution (Standard Deviational Ellipse) of auto theft
# locations.
arcpy.stats.DirectionalDistribution(locations, standardEllipse,
                                    "1_STANDARD_DEVIATION")

# Process: Linear Directional Mean of auto thefts.
arcpy.stats.DirectionalMean(links, linearDirectMean, "DIRECTION")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/directional-distribution.html#parameters

## 方向分布 (標準差橢圓) (空間統計工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 度量地理分布工具集

**english**　Directional Distribution (Standard Deviational Ellipse)

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/directional-distribution.html

**id**　c589c21e8e82715d7f-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/directional-distribution.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Directional Distribution (Standard Deviational Ellipse)，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入圖徵類 [Input_Feature_Class]、輸出橢圓圖徵類 [Output_Ellipse_Feature_Class]、橢圓大小 [Ellipse_Size]、權重欄位 （可選） [Weight_Field]、案例分組欄位 （可選） [Case_Field]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/directional-distribution.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "Input_Feature_Class", "label_zh": "輸入圖徵類", "label_en": "Input Feature Class", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "A feature class containing a distribution of features for which the standard deviational ellipse or ellipsoid will be calculated."}, {"parameter": "Output_Ellipse_Feature_Class", "label_zh": "輸出橢圓圖徵類", "label_en": "Output Ellipse Feature Class", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "A polygon feature class that will contain the output ellipse feature."}, {"parameter": "Ellipse_Size", "label_zh": "橢圓大小", "label_en": "Ellipse Size", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the size of the output ellipses in standard deviations. 1 standard deviation—The size of the output ellipses will be one standard deviation. This is the default. 2 standard deviations—The size of the output ellipses will be two standard deviations. 3 standard deviations—The size of the output ellipses will be three standard deviations."}, {"parameter": "Weight_Field", "label_zh": "權重欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Weight Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The numeric field that will be used to weight locations according to their relative importance."}, {"parameter": "Case_Field", "label_zh": "案例分組欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Case Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field that will be used to group features for separate directional distribution calculations. The case field can be of integer, date, or string type."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.stats.DirectionalDistribution(Input_Feature_Class, Output_Ellipse_Feature_Class, Ellipse_Size, {Weight_Field}, {Case_Field})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：Input_Feature_Class、Output_Ellipse_Feature_Class、Ellipse_Size、Weight_Field、Case_Field。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

**text**　Creates obstruction identification surfaces (OIS) pursuant to the Helipad Surface specification of the UFC 3-260-01 Section 4.4 HELIPADS, prescribed by MIL-STD 3007. These surfaces provide planning, design, construction, sustainment, restoration, and modernization criteria for the United States Department of Defense. Surfaces are created as polygon or multipatch features.

**id**　c58ef398699bf3bf3a-2

**english**　UFC Heliport

**text**　The tool creates United Facilities Criteria (UFC) surfaces in an existing polygon or a multipatch feature class. The Target OIS Features parameter value must have a vertical spatial reference.
To create a .json file for the Custom JSON File parameter, use the CustomizeOIS.exe utility included in the Aviation Airports Product Data download available from My Esri.
OIS tools are flexible and accept a variety of input and output feature class types. For more information, see the OIS Input and Output schemas.

## UFC Heliport (Aviation Tools) · 參數

**id**　c58ef398699bf3bf3a-6

**english**　UFC Heliport

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱

- 直升機場障礙標識表面工具集

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/ufc-heliport.html

**parameter**　surface_classification

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- Surface Classification
- Specifies the rotary-wing type of the aircraft. Standard VFR Helipad Army Airforce—Army and Air Force standard visual flight rules Helipad. Standard VFR Helipad Navy Marine or Limited VFR Army Airforce—Navy and Marine Corps standard visual flight rules Helipad or Army and Air Force limited visual flight rules. Standard IFR Helipad Army Airforce—Army and Air Force standard instrument flight rules Helipad. Standard IFR Helipad Navy Marine—Navy and Marine Corps standard instrument flight rules Helipad. Standard VFR Helipad Same Direction Army Airforce—Army and Air Force standard visual flight rules same direction Helipad. Limited VFR Helipad Same Direction Army Airforce—Army and Air Force limited visual flight rules same direction Helipad. Standard IFR Helipad Same Direction Army Airforce—Army and Air Force standard instrument flight rules same direction Helipad. Elevated UH60 or Small Helicopters—Elevated UH60 or small Helicopters. Elevated CH47 and Large Helicopters—Elevated CH47 and large Helicopters. Hospital Elevated UH60 or Small Helicopters—Hospital elevated UH60 or small Helicopters. Hospital Elevated CH47 and Large Helicopters—Hospital elevated CH47 and large Helicopters. Hoverpoints—A hoverpoint is a prepared and marked surface used as a reference or control point for air traffic control purposes by arriving or departing helicopters.
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/ufc-heliport.html#parameters

- surface_classification
- Specifies the rotary-wing type of the aircraft. STANDARD_VFR_HELIPAD_ARMY_AIRFORCE—Army and Air Force standard visual flight rules Helipad. STANDARD_VFR_HELIPAD_NAVY_MARINE_OR_LIMITED_VFR_ARMY_AIRFORCE—Navy and Marine Corps standard visual flight rules Helipad or Army and Air Force limited visual flight rules. STANDARD_IFR_HELIPAD_ARMY_AIRFORCE—Army and Air Force standard instrument flight rules Helipad. STANDARD_IFR_HELIPAD_NAVY_MARINE—Navy and Marine Corps standard instrument flight rules Helipad. STANDARD_VFR_HELIPAD_SAME_DIRECTION_ARMY_AIRFORCE—Army and Air Force standard visual flight rules same direction Helipad. LIMITED_VFR_HELIPAD_SAME_DIRECTION_ARMY_AIRFORCE—Army and Air Force limited visual flight rules same direction Helipad. STANDARD_IFR_HELIPAD_SAME_DIRECTION_ARMY_AIRFORCE—Army and Air Force standard instrument flight rules same direction Helipad. ELEVATED_UH_60_OR_SMALL_HELICOPTERS—Elevated UH60 or small Helicopters. ELEVATED_CH_47_AND_LARGE_HELICOPTERS—Elevated CH47 and large Helicopters. HOSPITAL_ELEVATED_UH_60_OR_SMALL_HELICOPTERS—Hospital elevated UH60 or small Helicopters. HOSPITAL_ELEVATED_CH_47_AND_LARGE_HELICOPTERS—Hospital elevated CH47 and large Helicopters. HOVERPOINTS—A hoverpoint is a prepared and marked surface used as a reference or control point for air traffic control purposes by arriving or departing helicopters.

## UFC Heliport (Aviation Tools) · 代碼示例

**id**　c58ef398699bf3bf3a-20

**english**　UFC Heliport

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 機場工具集
- 直升機場障礙標識表面工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/ufc-heliport.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/ufc-heliport.html

**text**　# Name: UFCHeliport_sample.py
# Description: Creates obstruction identification surfaces (OIS) for heliports
# based on the Unified Facilities Criteria (UFC) 3-260-01

import arcpy

input_fato_features = r'C:\Data\Airports.gdb\Helipads'
target_ois_features = r'C:\Data\Airports.gdb\ObstructionIdSurface_MP'
surface_classification = "HOVERPOINTS"
approach_bearing = 90.0
heliport_elevation = 250.0
custom_json_file = r"D:\customOIS.json"

#Call the UFCHeliport tool

arcpy.aviation.UFCHeliport(input_fato_features, target_ois_features, surface_classification, approach_bearing, heliport_elevation, custom_json_file)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/ufc-heliport.html#derived-output

## UFC Heliport (Aviation Tools) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 機場工具集
- 直升機場障礙標識表面工具集

**english**　UFC Heliport

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/ufc-heliport.html

**id**　c58ef398699bf3bf3a-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/ufc-heliport.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 UFC Heliport，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。Input FATO Features [input_fato_features]、Target OIS Features [target_ois_features]、Surface Classification [surface_classification]、Approach Bearing （可選） [approach_bearing]、Heliport Elevation （可選） [helipad_elevation]、Custom JSON File （可選） [custom_json_file]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/ufc-heliport.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "input_fato_features", "label_zh": "Input FATO Features", "label_en": "Input FATO Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The input Final Approach and Takeoff (FATO) features."}, {"parameter": "target_ois_features", "label_zh": "Target OIS Features", "label_en": "Target OIS Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The target feature class that will contain the generated OIS."}, {"parameter": "surface_classification", "label_zh": "Surface Classification", "label_en": "Surface Classification", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the rotary-wing type of the aircraft. Standard VFR Helipad Army Airforce—Army and Air Force standard visual flight rules Helipad. Standard VFR Helipad Navy Marine or Limited VFR Army Airforce—Navy and Marine Corps standard visual flight rules Helipad or Army and Air Force limited visual flight rules. Standard IFR Helipad Army Airforce—Army and Air Force standard instrument flight rules Helipad. Standard IFR Helipad Navy Marine—Navy and Marine Corps standard instrument flight rules Helipad. Standard VFR Helipad Same Direction Army Airforce—Army and Air Force standard visual flight rules same direction Helipad. Limited VFR Helipad Same Direction Army Airforce—Army and Air Force limited visual flight rules same direction Helipad. Standard IFR Helipad Same Direction Army Airforce—Army and Air Force standard instrument flight rules same direction Helipad. Elevated UH60 or Small Helicopters—Elevated UH60 or small Helicopters. Elevated CH47 and Large Helicopters—Elevated CH47 and large Helicopters. Hospital Elevated UH60 or Small Helicopters—Hospital elevated UH60 or small Helicopters. Hospital Elevated CH47 and Large Helicopters—Hospital elevated CH47 and large Helicopters. Hoverpoints—A hoverpoint is a prepared and marked surface used as a reference or control point for air traffic control purposes by arriving or departing helicopters."}, {"parameter": "approach_bearing", "label_zh": "Approach Bearing （可選）", "label_en": "Approach Bearing (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The absolute bearing that an approaching aircraft will travel along the surface. A value of 0 will align the surface to true north. The default value is 0."}, {"parameter": "helipad_elevation", "label_zh": "Heliport Elevation （可選）", "label_en": "Heliport Elevation (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The elevation of the highest point of the heliport. The value must be in the vertical coordinate system linear units of the target feature class. The default is 0."}, {"parameter": "custom_json_file", "label_zh": "Custom JSON File （可選）", "label_en": "Custom JSON File (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "File", "description_en": "The import configuration, in JSON format, that will be used to create the custom OIS."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.aviation.UFCHeliport(input_fato_features, target_ois_features, surface_classification, {approach_bearing}, {helipad_elevation}, {custom_json_file})

## 網格計算器的工作原理 · 網格計算器的工作原理

**id**　c5965de07a81e71c5a-1

**english**　How Raster Calculator works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 地圖代數工具集
- 地圖代數工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-raster-calculator-works.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-raster-calculator-works.html

**text**　需具備 Spatial Analyst 許可方可使用。
需具有 Image Analyst 許可才能使用。
網格計算器工具用于創建和運行地圖代數表達式。
與其他地理處理工具一樣，網格計算器工具可以在 ModelBuilder 中使用，從而將地圖代數的強大功能納入工作流中。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-raster-calculator-works.html

## 網格計算器的工作原理 · 注:

**id**　c5965de07a81e71c5a-2

**english**　How Raster Calculator works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 地圖代數工具集
- 地圖代數工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-raster-calculator-works.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-raster-calculator-works.html

**text**　網格計算器工具不適合用于腳本環境中，而且不能在標準的 Spatial Analyst ArcPy 模塊中使用。 要了解關于腳本和地圖代數的詳細信息，請參閱地圖代數。
網格計算器工具具有如下優勢：
執行單行代數表達式。
使用 ModelBuilder 時，支持在地圖代數中使用變量。
在單個表達式中對三個或更多的輸入應用 Spatial Analyst 運算符。
在一個表達式中使用多個 Spatial Analyst 工具。
網格計算器工具用于通過多種工具和運算符來運行單行代數表達式。 當在一個表達式中使用多種工具或運算符時，該公式的執行速度一般會比分別運行每個運算符或工具的速度要快。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-raster-calculator-works.html

## 網格計算器的工作原理 · 工具

**id**　c5965de07a81e71c5a-5

**english**　How Raster Calculator works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 地圖代數工具集
- 地圖代數工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-raster-calculator-works.html#846

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-raster-calculator-works.html

**text**　在工具列表中，可以選擇能在地圖代數表達式中使用的工具。 當單擊列表中的工具時，該工具的名稱以及括號的左右半邊 () 將被放置在表達式中指針當前所在的位置。 然后必須輸入工具所需的剩余輸入。 工具可以放置在表達式中的任何位置，但其放置位置應可以生成有效的地圖代數語法。
列表中的運算符允許您在表達式中輸入數學（加法、除法等）和邏輯（大于、等于等）運算符。 當選擇這些符號時，可以在表達式中指針當前所在的位置輸入運算符。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-raster-calculator-works.html#846

## 網格計算器的工作原理 · 地圖代數語言

**id**　c5965de07a81e71c5a-7

**english**　How Raster Calculator works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 地圖代數工具集
- 地圖代數工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-raster-calculator-works.html#8C1

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-raster-calculator-works.html

**text**　地圖代數是一種簡單而強大的代數語言，利用它您可以運行全部 Spatial Analyst 工具、運算符及函數以執行地理分析。
在網格計算器中使用的地圖代數含有語法或規則集，地圖代數必須遵循這些語法或規則集才能創建有效的表達式。 如果不遵守這些規則，則表達式可能無效并且不會運行，或者您可能無法獲得預期結果。
不僅可以通過網格計算器工具來訪問“地圖代數”，還可以通過使用 Spatial Analyst ArcPy 模塊的 Python 腳本來訪問。 在此工具中使用相同的地圖代數語法，但以下情況除外：
不必將輸出網格名稱或等號 (=) 填入表達式，因為該輸出名稱已在輸出網格參數中指定。
在使用運算符時，不必將輸入資料轉換為 Raster 對象。
有關地圖代數的詳細信息，請參閱以下內容：
使用地圖代數簡介
地圖代數的規則

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-raster-calculator-works.html#8C1

## 填洼的工作原理 · 填洼的工作原理

**id**　c5d2d9f87648354a08-1

**english**　How Fill works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 水文分析工具集
- 水文分析工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-fill-works.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-fill-works.html

**text**　需具備 Spatial Analyst 許可方可使用。
洼地（和突起）是由于資料的分辨率或將高程四舍五入到最近的整數值而產生的常見錯誤。
應對洼地進行填充以確保盆地和河流得以正確劃界。 如果未對洼地進行填充，則生成的水系網絡可能會呈現不連續性。
填洼工具使用與焦點流、流向、匯、集水區和區域填充等工具等效的功能來定位和填充洼地。 該工具的執行過程會進行迭代，直到指定 z 限制內的所有洼地均填充完畢。 在填充洼地的同時，可能會在填充區域的邊界處創建其他洼地，這些洼地將在下個迭代中移除。
該工具還可以用于去除峰值，根據周圍表面的趨勢，這些峰值為高程比預期值更大的偽面像元。
對于非 SDTS USGS 30 米分辨率的 DEM，Tarboton 等人 (1991) 發現 DEM 中 0.9% 到 4.7% 的像元是洼地。 這些洼地的平均校正范圍為 2.6 到 4.8 米。 這意味著對于一個 1,000 × 1,000 的像元格網（100 萬個像元），可能有 9,000 到 47,000 個洼地點要填充。 其他 DEM 資料源中洼地所占的百分比會因表面處理方式的不同而或高或低。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-fill-works.html

**chapter**

- 管理工具集

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/parcel/upgradearcmapparcelfabric.html

**text**　將 ArcMap 宗地結構升級為 ArcGIS Pro 宗地結構。
該工具可在圖徵資料集中創建宗地結構，并將資料從 ArcMap 宗地結構圖徵類傳輸到 ArcGIS Pro 宗地結構圖徵類。 現有的 ArcMap 宗地結構不會更改。
了解有關升級 ArcMap 宗地結構的詳細信息

**content_version**　3.7

## 升級 ArcMap 宗地結構 (宗地工具) · 用法

**id**　c5d4c32af24fd88fad-2

**english**　Upgrade ArcMap Parcel Fabric

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 宗地工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/parcel/upgradearcmapparcelfabric.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/parcel/upgradearcmapparcelfabric.html

**text**　該工具可用于升級文件地理資料庫、企業級地理資料庫或移動地理資料庫上的 ArcMap 宗地結構。
此工具可在已升級的宗地結構上啟用拓撲。
ArcMap 宗地圖徵類的 Type 欄位中定義的宗地類型可用于在 ArcGIS Pro 宗地結構中創建宗地類型。 如果 Type 欄位使用屬性域，則域名將被指定為宗地類型名稱。 如果 Type 欄位沒有屬性域，則宗地類型將按照 Type 欄位中為其分配的編號進行命名。
輸出圖徵資料集僅可包含一個宗地結構。
在企業級地理資料庫中，此工具必須在默認版本中執行。
在企業級地理資料庫中，輸出圖徵資料集必須來自作為經資料庫身份驗證的用戶建立的資料庫連接。 此用戶必須具有創建內容的資料庫權限且未經 OS 驗證，且不能為資料庫管理員或地理資料庫管理員賬戶。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/parcel/upgradearcmapparcelfabric.html#usage

## 升級 ArcMap 宗地結構 (宗地工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 宗地工具箱
- 管理工具集

**english**　Upgrade ArcMap Parcel Fabric

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/parcel/upgradearcmapparcelfabric.html

**id**　c5d4c32af24fd88fad-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/parcel/upgradearcmapparcelfabric.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Upgrade ArcMap Parcel Fabric，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。ArcMap 的輸入宗地結構 [in_parcel_fabric]、目標圖徵資料集 [target_dataset]、名稱 [name]、刪除相同重疊線 （可選） [delete_identical]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/parcel/upgradearcmapparcelfabric.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_parcel_fabric", "label_zh": "ArcMap 的輸入宗地結構", "label_en": "Input Parcel Fabric For ArcMap", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Parcel Fabric Layer for ArcMap", "description_en": "The ArcMap parcel fabric that will be upgraded to an ArcGIS Pro parcel fabric."}, {"parameter": "target_dataset", "label_zh": "目標圖徵資料集", "label_en": "Target Feature Dataset", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Dataset", "description_en": "The feature dataset that will contain the upgraded ArcGIS Pro parcel fabric."}, {"parameter": "name", "label_zh": "名稱", "label_en": "Name", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the upgraded ArcGIS Pro parcel fabric."}, {"parameter": "delete_identical", "label_zh": "刪除相同重疊線 （可選）", "label_en": "Delete identical overlapping lines (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether identical overlapping lines will be deleted. When the parameter is checked, overlapping lines will be deleted if the line shapes are identical (lines are coincident) and they have the following matching attributes: Directions in the Direction field. This includes directions that are reversed by 180 degrees. Distances in the Distance field. Distances are rounded to four decimal places. Records in the Created By Record field. Records in the Retired By Record field. Checked—Identical overlapping lines will be deleted. Unchecked—Identical overlapping lines will not be deleted. This is the default."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.parcel.UpgradeArcMapParcelFabric(in_parcel_fabric, target_dataset, name, {delete_identical})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_parcel_fabric、target_dataset、name、delete_identical。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 110373: 所有特征值均接近零。 · 解決方案

**text**　雖然此錯誤可能會發生，但發生的頻率非常低，以至于我們尚且無法確定典型原因，也因此目前沒有可用的解決方案。
如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/160001-170000/tool-errors-and-warnings-160751-160775-160753.html#solution

**id**　c5edf70e8191f68fb0-1

**english**　How Pairwise Intersect works

- 地理處理工具

**text**　成對相交工具使用成對比較技術計算兩個圖徵圖層或圖徵類中圖徵之間的交集。 工具輸出兩個輸入所共有的圖徵或圖徵的一部分。

## 成對相交的工作原理 · 相交和成對相交工具比較

**id**　c5edf70e8191f68fb0-2

**english**　How Pairwise Intersect works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 分析工具箱
- 成對疊加工具集
- 成對疊加工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/analysis/how-pairwise-intersect-works.html#BAC

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/analysis/how-pairwise-intersect-works.html

**text**　使用成對相交工具時，第一個輸入中的一個圖徵與第二個輸入中的每個單獨圖徵相交。 這與相交工具的工作方式根本不同。 使用相交工具，所有圖徵都會相交，無論該圖徵屬于哪個輸入。
相交工具用于執行以下操作：
與多個圖徵圖層或圖徵類相交
查找所有輸入圖徵的交集，無論它們屬于哪個輸入，創建表示所有圖徵幾何交集的拓撲結構
成對相交工具用于執行以下操作：
僅接受兩個圖徵圖層或圖徵類作為輸入
從第一個輸入中獲取圖徵，并將它們與第二個輸入中的每個圖徵逐個相交，創建代表其交集的新圖徵

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/analysis/how-pairwise-intersect-works.html#BAC

## 成對相交的工作原理 · 相交工具

**id**　c5edf70e8191f68fb0-3

**english**　How Pairwise Intersect works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 分析工具箱
- 成對疊加工具集
- 成對疊加工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/analysis/how-pairwise-intersect-works.html#FD0

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/analysis/how-pairwise-intersect-works.html

**text**　所有圖徵之間的所有交集都會被計算，無論它們屬于什么輸入。 由于所有交集都經過計算，因此輸出所包含的圖徵數量可能比所有輸入圖徵的總數量多得多。 例如，兩個輸入包含數萬個圖徵，由于所有圖徵之間存在復雜的重疊，因此可能會產生數億個圖徵的輸出。
例如，下面顯示了兩個圖徵圖層；第一個包含 10 個圖徵，第二個包含 1 個圖徵。
下圖顯示了使用兩個輸入的相交工具的結果。 無論是哪個輸入，都會計算所有圖徵的交點。 這可以產生比輸入中發現的更多的圖徵。 在這種情況下，輸出中的圖徵 (167) 比兩個輸入中的圖徵 (11) 更多。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/analysis/how-pairwise-intersect-works.html#FD0

**id**　c5edf70e8191f68fb0-4

**english**　How Pairwise Intersect works

下圖顯示了使用兩個輸入的成對相交工具的結果。 輸出僅包括 10 個圖徵。
